| AMB-159 | Employee defence & hearings | Should the subject see the evidence against them for the defence?               | Disclosure may expose the reporter; non-disclosure may defeat the defence. | Q90; ADR-024 (notice snapshots allegations only); §22; ADR-041 §5; ADR-041 §8; ADR-042 §8 (migration 2050, PR #74) | R5               | Decided by the owner on 2026-10-13, option C: "Permit selected evidence through a purpose-bound Defence Packet, following review/redaction for reporter, witness, confidentiality, privilege, restricted information and other protected identities/content. This does not create general evidence access for the subject." | No                                                     | CRITICAL | Decided 2026-10-13 (DEC-AMB-006) | Decided; built in migration 2050 (ADR-041 §5; ADR-041 §8; ADR-042 §8), on main since PR #74 (merged 2026-10-14). Migration 2050 builds the Defence Packet. There is one open `grievance.defence_packet` per DEFENCE notice. The case's evidence leads choose the items (`api.add_defence_packet_item`, originals of that case only). A different lead reviews each item (`api.review_defence_packet_item`) across REPORTER, WITNESS, CONFIDENTIALITY, PRIVILEGE, RESTRICTED_INFORMATION and OTHER_PROTECTED, then clears it as is, clears a redacted rendition of it, or withholds it. `api.submit_defence_packet` refuses `ITEM_NOT_REVIEWED`, `NOTHING_CLEARED` and `ITEM_NOT_DISCLOSABLE`. A restricted case's packet waits for its Restricted Case Authority (`api.decide_restricted_defence_packet`, `REQUESTER_CANNOT_CONSENT`). The subject sees only cleared items of released packets, under their reviewed titles (`api.my_defence_packet`, and `api.open_defence_packet_item` served INLINE). The subject never reaches the evidence tables, storage or the download gate, and the notice itself still carries no evidence or reporter field. Defaults: `reviewer_must_differ` is true (`REVIEWER_IS_CHOOSER`) and `packet_evidence_download` is false. LEGAL_PRIVILEGE_RULE ships SOURCE_REQUIRED, so clearing an item is refused with `LEGAL_PRIVILEGE_RULE_REQUIRED` and nothing reaches the subject until the rule is recorded. Owner: Rebase PR-B, 2050 (P11-G4 evidence half). | T-AMB-092: A defence notice never carries evidence or reporter fields. Evidence reaches the subject only as items selected into a purpose-bound Defence Packet after the review and redaction for reporter, witness, confidentiality, privilege, restricted information and other protected identities; an unreviewed item is refused, a restricted case also needs its Restricted Case Authority, and the packet gives no general evidence access. `tests/security/grievance-defence.spec.ts` (EXISTS, negative; note: Re-checked on main 0f59062: "T-AMB-092: evidence reaches the subject only as reviewed Defence Packet items; a restricted case also needs its authority" (PR #74, merged 2026-10-14).) |
| AMB-160 | Employee defence & hearings | How is the reporter protected when allegation text goes to the subject?         | Allegation text can identify the reporter.                                 | ADR-024 note for the UI; §22                                                                                       | R1               | Notices snapshot only the allegation text the issuer chose; the issuing screen warns about identifying content; protected identity is never a notice field.                                                                                                                                                                 | No                                                     | CRITICAL | No                               | On-screen warning before allegation text is sent to the subject. Owner: UX thread, Phase 12 screens.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | T-AMB-093: Protected identity never appears in a notice (negative, N19). `tests/security/identity-leakage.spec.ts (new case)` (NEW, negative; note: the vault scan already covers the grievance schema, but no notice is seeded for it to scan)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### N. Evidence

| ID      | Domain   | Ambiguity                                                              | Why It Matters                                                               | Existing Evidence                                                                                          | Resolution Class | Proposed Resolution                                                                                                                                | Configurable?                                        | Risk     | Owner Question Needed?                    | Implementation Impact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Test Required                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| AMB-161 | Evidence | How is withdrawn or excluded (inadmissible) evidence handled?          | Deleting evidence breaks custody; using excluded evidence taints a decision. | DEC-F37; ADR-039 (evidence half P11-G4 open); ADR-006; ADR-041 §1 (migration 2050, PR #74)                 | R1               | Exclusion with reason and audit; the item stays immutable and is left out of packets and exports.                                                  | No                                                   | HIGH     | No                                        | Built in migration 2050 (ADR-041 §1), on main since PR #74 (merged 2026-10-14). Migration 2050 adds `api.exclude_evidence(evidence, reason)` for an evidence lead on an ACTIVE case. It refuses `CASE_NOT_ACTIVE`, and `EVIDENCE_EXCLUDED` if the item is already excluded. It writes a final, append-only `evidence.exclusion` row (numbered within the case, with a reason of 10-2000 characters), an `EXCLUDED` custody event and an `EVIDENCE_EXCLUDED` audit event; `api.evidence_exclusion_record` produces the formal record. The item, its versions and its custody stay as they are. Triggers refuse any new version or rendition of an excluded item and any Defence Packet item made from it (`EVIDENCE_EXCLUDED`). The legal review packet never carries evidence, and exports of evidence are still refused entirely (T-AMB-111). Owner: Rebase PR-B, 2050 (P11-G4 evidence half).                                                                                                                                   | T-AMB-094: Excluded evidence cannot be added to a packet (negative). `tests/security/evidence-need-to-know.spec.ts` (EXISTS, negative; note: Re-checked on main 0f59062: "T-AMB-094: excluded evidence cannot be added to a packet, by the command or on the table" (PR #74, merged 2026-10-14).)                          |
| AMB-162 | Evidence | Can evidence be replaced?                                              | Overwrite destroys the original.                                             | ADR-006 (immutable versions); §24–§27                                                                      | R1               | Only by a new version; the original stays.                                                                                                         | No                                                   | HIGH     | No                                        | None (built).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | T-AMB-095: No update of a stored version is possible (negative). `tests/security/evidence-access.spec.ts` (EXISTS, negative)                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| AMB-163 | Evidence | Does a legal hold affect evidence?                                     | Destruction during a hold is unlawful.                                       | ADR-013 D4–D5; DEC-F33                                                                                     | R1               | A hold blocks every destructive or downgrading change; access and work continue unless a hold instruction says otherwise.                          | No                                                   | CRITICAL | No                                        | None (built).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | T-AMB-096: Disposition and downgrades are refused under hold; a new version is still accepted (negative). `tests/security/records-legal-hold.spec.ts; tests/security/records-disposition.spec.ts` (EXISTS, negative)                                                                                                       |
| AMB-164 | Evidence | How is a chain-of-custody break or an external forensic copy recorded? | Silent repair invalidates the evidence.                                      | ADR-006 (custody events); ADR-041 §2 (migration 2050, PR #74)                                              | R2               | As custody events (EXTERNAL_TRANSFER, CUSTODY_EXCEPTION) with reason; the item is flagged; nothing is rewritten.                                   | No                                                   | HIGH     | No                                        | Built in migration 2050 (ADR-041 §2), on main since PR #74 (merged 2026-10-14). Migration 2050 adds `api.record_custody_exception(evidence, version, kind, reason, external_reference)` for the evidence team holding EVIDENCE_UPLOAD, on an ACTIVE or CLOSED case (otherwise `RECORDS_READ_ONLY`). The kind is CUSTODY_EXCEPTION or EXTERNAL_TRANSFER; an external transfer needs its external reference, and every record needs a reason. Each call writes a custody event of that kind and an append-only `evidence.custody_exception` row: no update, delete or truncate, and `evidence.guard_custody_exception` checks that the row points at its own custody event. It is audited `EVIDENCE_CUSTODY_EXCEPTION_RECORDED`. The item is flagged by the row's presence (`custody_flagged` in `api.defence_packet_view`), and nothing is rewritten. Owner: Rebase PR-B, 2050 (P11-G4 evidence half).                                                                                                                             | T-AMB-097: A custody exception cannot be removed once recorded. `tests/security/evidence-access.spec.ts` (EXISTS; note: Re-checked on main 0f59062: "T-AMB-097: a custody exception cannot be removed once recorded; an external transfer names its reference" (PR #74, merged 2026-10-14).)                               |
| AMB-165 | Evidence | What happens when a stored file no longer matches its hash?            | Tampering or corruption must not reach a decision.                           | ADR-006 (hash per version); ADR-041 §3 (migration 2050, PR #74)                                            | R2               | The version is quarantined, downloads are blocked, a security event is raised and the case owner is notified by template code.                     | No                                                   | CRITICAL | No                                        | Built in migration 2050 (ADR-041 §3), on main since PR #74 (merged 2026-10-14). The BFF (`packages/application/src/evidence.ts`) computes the SHA-256 of the stored bytes before serving them; on a mismatch it serves nothing, returns a generic `EVIDENCE_INTEGRITY_FAILED` error with the correlation id, and calls `api.report_evidence_integrity_failure(version, observed)`. That command writes the append-only `evidence.integrity_failure` row, an `INTEGRITY_FAILED` custody event, a SECURITY event `EVIDENCE_INTEGRITY_FAILED`, and tells the case owner by template code `EVIDENCE_INTEGRITY_FAILED` (ids only). From then on `api.open_evidence_version` refuses the version to every reader with the same SECURITY denial as a missing version (`EVIDENCE_ACCESS_DENIED`, metadata `integrity_failed`), no rendition can be made from it (`CONFLICT:INTEGRITY_FAILED`), and it cannot enter or stay visible in a Defence Packet (`evidence.version_disclosable`). | AMB-169 | Evidence | How is evidence redacted for a packet or export?                       | Redaction must not alter the original.                                       | ADR-006; DEC-09; ADR-041 §4; ADR-041 §5 (migration 2050, PR #74)                                           | R2               | A redacted rendition is a new derived version linked to the original; packets use it where the purpose requires.                                   | No                                                   | HIGH     | No                                        | Built in migration 2050 (ADR-041 §4; ADR-041 §5), on main since PR #74 (merged 2026-10-14). An evidence lead registers a redacted rendition with `api.register_evidence_rendition`: a new REDACTED version linked to its original by `derived_from_version_id`, through the same quarantine, scan and `api.complete_evidence_version` pipeline. It never becomes the item's current version, the original is never changed, there is no rendition of a rendition (`CONFLICT:NOT_AN_ORIGINAL`), and a forged row must name an intact original of the same item (trigger `evidence.guard_rendition`, `CONFLICT:RENDITION_SOURCE_INVALID`). A Defence Packet reviewer can clear an item as `CLEARED_REDACTED`, disclosing the rendition instead of the original. When the outbox marks a mandatory employee-notice email (`DEFENCE_NOTICE_ISSUED`, `RIGHTS_NOTICE_ISSUED`, `DECISION_NOTICE_ISSUED`) FAILED after its fifth attempt, the trigger `notify.on_notice_email_failed` opens a `grievance.notice_delivery_task` assigned to the notice's issuer, once per failed message and only for an open, undelivered notice, audited `NOTICE_DELIVERY_TASK_CREATED` (SYSTEM, ids only). `api.record_notice_delivery` closes the open tasks; the in-app copy is a separate outbox row and stays. Not built: No failure or bounce code is recorded: `notify.outbox` (1920) keeps only the FAILED status and the attempt count, and `notify.record_email_delivery` takes only delivered true or false. Tracked as CDF-244, placed in migration 2170. For corrective actions, `decision.corrective_action_policy` gains `reminder_repeat_days` and `overdue_after_days`, and `decision.corrective_action_escalation_step` holds up to ten steps (after N days overdue, to the OWNER, CASE_MANAGERS or a ROLE), all set with the policy version through the 12-argument `api.set_corrective_action_policy` (CORRECTIVE_ACTION_POLICY_ADMIN). Every value ships null and no step is seeded (the 1940 policies stay SOURCE_REQUIRED placeholders), so nothing is reminded or escalated until configured; `notify.emit_due_reminders` sends `CORRECTIVE_ACTION_DUE`/`CORRECTIVE_ACTION_OVERDUE` only to recipients who pass the case gate. It is built as policy columns and a step table, not as REMINDER_CADENCE and ESCALATION_PATH settings. Export policy is administered only by `authz.is_export_policy_admin` (EXPORT_POLICY_ADMIN without EXPORT_APPROVE or EXPORT_SECOND_APPROVE; granted to PLATFORM_ADMIN). `exchange.eligible_approvers` and the trigger `exchange.guard_export_approval` never admit the requester and, once a stage's `exchange.export_approver_pool` is in use, only its current members (FORBIDDEN otherwise). When a submitted export's next stage has no independent eligible approver it waits (fail closed) and `exchange.warn_unapprovable_export` writes `exchange.export_approval_warning` and audits `EXPORT_APPROVAL_CONFIGURATION_WARNING` (ids and counts), read through `api.export_approval_config_warnings`. The pool is a table kept with `api.add_export_approver` and `api.revoke_export_approver`, not an EXPORT_SECOND_APPROVER_POOL setting. | AMB-212 | Closure, archive & records | Who initiates and who approves case closure?                          | Unauthorised closure ends investigations.                                     | DEC-F40; P11-G2 scoping; ADR-042 §1; ADR-042 §4; ADR-042 §5; ADR-043 §1 (migrations 2060 and 2070, PR #77, PR #80)                            | R4               | Initiated by the case owner or authorised role; approval routed per the DoA, which is SOURCE_REQUIRED. Direct, maker/approver and returned closures all supported. DEC-AMB-002 (decided 2026-10-13, built in 2060) gives reopening to "the applicable closure authority", so this rule names that authority too. DEC-AMB-015 (decided 2026-10-13, built in 2060) gives the same authority the reopening for `DECISIVE_POST_DECISION_CONFLICT`: "The closure authority must not re-decide the conflict merits; it authorizes reopening once the conflict event has already been validated through the applicable conflict process. Fail closed if the required closure authority is not configured."                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Yes — `CASE_CLOSURE_APPROVAL_RULE = SOURCE_REQUIRED`    | HIGH     | No                                            | Built in migrations 2060 and 2070 (ADR-042 §1; ADR-042 §4; ADR-042 §5; ADR-043 §1), on main since PR #77 (merged 2026-10-14) and PR #80 (merged 2026-10-15). `api.close_case(case, outcome_code, rationale)` is initiated by a holder of `WORKFLOW_ADVANCE` within clearance and without a case conflict. `CASE_CLOSURE_APPROVAL_RULE` ships SOURCE_REQUIRED: closure stays direct, only where an enabled CDF_CASE_V1 step reaches CLOSURE (SCREEN_OUT, OUT_OF_JURISDICTION), otherwise `CASE_CLOSURE_APPROVAL_RULE_REQUIRED`. Once it names a role in force (`case_mgmt.closure_authority_role()`), that authority closes directly, anyone else files a closure request it approves or returns with `api.decide_case_closure` (never its maker: `REQUESTER_CANNOT_APPROVE`), the readiness trigger refuses any other path (`CLOSURE_APPROVAL_REQUIRED`), and the same authority decides every reopening, including `DECISIVE_POST_DECISION_CONFLICT`, failing closed while unset; since 2070 (CDF-221) a CONFIGURED rule naming no authority in force refuses closure on every path with `CASE_CLOSURE_APPROVAL_RULE_REQUIRED`. conditional closure path is used. (ADR-042 §3), on main since PR #77 (merged 2026-10-14) and PR #80 (merged 2026-10-15). `case_mgmt.closure_blocks(case)` and the BEFORE trigger `case_record_readiness` refuse every ACTIVE→CLOSED change while anything is open: the earlier `CORRECTIVE_ACTIONS_OUTSTANDING` (1940), `CONFLICT_REMEDIATION_OPEN`, `TAINTED_BY_CONFLICT` and `FROZEN_BY_SUPERSEDED_DECISION` guards, plus the new `OPEN_PROCEEDINGS`, `GRIEVANCE_WINDOW_OPEN` (a decision notice whose grievance window has not started or not ended) and `DECISION_NOT_FINAL` (an open grievance or reconsideration, or a package between the vote and the authority's decision). `api.close_case`, `api.decide_case_closure` and `api.transition_case` all hit the same blocks, and no conditional closure path exists (`CONDITIONAL_CLOSURE_AUTHORITY` is SOURCE_REQUIRED). §2), on main since PR #77 (merged 2026-10-14). `case_mgmt.case_outcome_code` is migration-managed reference data: 15 OUTCOME_CODE values taken from CDF's CRM closure statuses (`EMERGENCY_CLOSURE` inactive), separate from lifecycle status. Each closure made by `api.close_case` or `api.decide_case_closure` writes an append-only `case_mgmt.case_closure` row with the outcome, rationale and path, leaving proceeding outcomes, findings, sanctions, corrective actions and grievances untouched. `CASE_OUTCOME_CATALOGUE` ships SOURCE_REQUIRED (CDF confirmation of the values). Not built: A case closed through `api.transition_case` SCREEN_OUT or OUT_OF_JURISDICTION (allowed while CASE_CLOSURE_APPROVAL_RULE is SOURCE_REQUIRED) records no `case_closure` row and so no outcome. (migration 2060, PR #77)                                                                                                        | R2               | Yes at subject level; the case closes when every subject path is concluded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | No                                                      | MEDIUM   | No                                            | Partly built in migration 2060, on main since PR #77 (merged 2026-10-14). Subject paths already ran separately (per-subject defence and decision notices, grievance windows and grievances since 1910 and 1990). 2060 adds the case-level half: the case cannot close while any proceeding is open (`OPEN_PROCEEDINGS`), any decision notice's grievance window is open (`GRIEVANCE_WINDOW_OPEN`), or any grievance or decision is not final (`DECISION_NOT_FINAL`), through `case_mgmt.closure_blocks`. Not built: No subject-level path status or subject-level closure record: a subject's path is concluded only implicitly (its notice window ended, no grievance open), and proceedings are case-level, not per subject. Owner: Rebase PR-B, 2060 (P11-G2 case closure).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| AMB-218 | Closure, archive & records | Who may reopen a closed case and on what grounds?                     | Reopening without control undermines finality; refusing ignores new evidence. | ADR-013 D3 (reopen resets retention); P11-G2 scoping (REOPEN_CASE disabled); ADR-042 §5; ADR-042, "Not built here" (migration 2060, PR #77)   | R5               | Decided by the owner on 2026-10-13, option B: "A closed case may be reopened by the applicable closure authority based on new evidence, with documented reason, approval, full audit trail, and retention recalculation." Follow-on DEC-AMB-015, decided by the owner on 2026-10-13, option A: "Use the controlled reopening machinery of DEC-AMB-002, but treat the validated post-decision conflict event as an authorized reopening trigger alongside new evidence. The workflow is: `CLOSED CASE` → validated decisive conflict event → closure authority reviews reopening request → reopening approved with reason and audit → case reopens → affected decision chain marked `TAINTED_BY_CONFLICT` → committee reconvenes → quorum and eligibility recalculated → re-vote without conflicted member → competent authority re-decides → former authority decision remains immutable and becomes `SUPERSEDED` → downstream consequences reconciled → case may proceed toward closure again. This does not mean the conflict itself is treated as “new evidence.” Instead: `DEC-AMB-002 reopening infrastructure` is reused as the controlled reopening gate, while the reopening reason type is: `DECISIVE_POST_DECISION_CONFLICT` Add this as a distinct governed reopening reason. The closure authority must not re-decide the conflict merits; it authorizes reopening once the conflict event has already been validated through the applicable conflict process. Fail closed if the required closure authority is not configured." | No                                                      | HIGH     | Decided 2026-10-13 (DEC-AMB-002, DEC-AMB-015) | Decided; partly built in migration 2060 (ADR-042 §5; ADR-042, "Not built here"), on main since PR #77 (merged 2026-10-14). The decided rule's reopening gate is built, replacing the old fail-safe: `REOPEN_CASE` is now an enabled system step that `api.transition_case` refuses, and only `api.decide_case_reopening` runs it after the closure authority approves. `api.request_case_reopening` takes reason `NEW_EVIDENCE` (description, reference and justification required) or the distinct `DECISIVE_POST_DECISION_CONFLICT` (a decided package whose validated conflict could have changed the vote, else `NO_VALIDATED_DECISIVE_CONFLICT`; never recorded as evidence), fails closed with `CASE_CLOSURE_APPROVAL_RULE_REQUIRED` while the authority is unset, and the maker never decides (`REQUESTER_CANNOT_APPROVE`). Approval is audited (`CASE_REOPENING_REQUESTED`, `CASE_REOPENING_DECIDED`, `CASE_REOPENED`), returns the case to ACTIVE in INVESTIGATION and supersedes its retention schedule as REOPENED for recalculation; the authority decision is never edited and the tainted package keeps the case from closing (`TAINTED_BY_CONFLICT`). Not built: The rest of DEC-AMB-015's chain after a conflict reopening: reconvening the concluded committee proceeding for the re-vote (not designed, ADR-042), so the re-decision, SUPERSEDED marking and reconciliation are not reachable from a reopened case; and a reopened case has no step into CLOSURE yet (`CLOSURE_STEP_NOT_AVAILABLE`). Owner: Rebase PR-B, 2060 (P11-G2 case closure). | T-AMB-131: Only the applicable closure authority can reopen a closed case, on new evidence with a documented reason and approval; the reopening is audited and recalculates retention, and a reopening by anyone else or without a reason is refused. For a decisive conflict found after closure (DEC-AMB-015): reopening for post-decision conflict requires closure-authority approval. `tests/security/workflow-bypass.spec.ts` (EXISTS, negative; note: Re-checked on main 0f59062: "T-AMB-131: only the closure authority reopens, on new evidence with a documented reason, never the maker; audited, retention recalculated" (PR #77, merged 2026-10-14).) |

No post-closure monitoring tasks exist, and there is no `POST_CLOSURE_MONITORING` setting. ADR-042 and the 2060 header list post-closure monitoring tasks (AMB-219) as not built. | AMB-240 | UI / UX | What does each role land on?                                | Landing by role leaks capabilities.         | ADR-016 D0; ADR-030; ADR-051 §2 (migration 2045, PR #73)                                                          | R1               | My Work, with areas derived from permission and relationship.                                                | No                                       | LOW    | No                                        | Built before the register was written, in migration 1820; extended in migration 2045 (ADR-051 §2), on main since PR #73 (merged 2026-10-14). `api.my_areas()` (1820) derives each sidebar area from permission and live relationship, never from a role name, with My Work first. The app lands on My Work, except that a records-only worker lands on Records (my-tasks) and a caller without My Work on their first area, such as Oversight (work-routes.ts `landingFor`). 2045 adds My Committees (`COMMITTEE`, default view `committees`) for a current seat on any committee. It widens Administration to the business administration permissions: `USER_ADMIN`, `ROLE_ADMIN`, `COMMITTEE_ADMIN`, `SECTOR_ADMIN`, `NOTIFICATION_ADMIN`, `authz.is_export_policy_admin()`, `CORRECTIVE_ACTION_POLICY_ADMIN` and `BUSINESS_CALENDAR_ADMIN`. | AMB-246 | UI / UX | How are SOURCE_REQUIRED values shown?                       | Placeholders could look like policy.        | UX gap map §5; appendix §32; ADR-051, "Context"; ADR-051 §1; ADR-054, "P12-2a amendment" (migration 2045, PR #73) | R1               | As configurable, never as CDF policy; due dates shown only when configured.                                  | No                                       | MEDIUM | No                                        | Partly built in migration 2045 (ADR-051, "Context"; ADR-051 §1; ADR-054, "P12-2a amendment"), on main since PR #73 (merged 2026-10-14). `api.my_work_items()` gives a Phase 12 row a due date only where one is configured or recorded, with its source in `due_source`, and null otherwise. The sources are `MINUTES_REVIEW` (always: `review_due_at` is NOT NULL, set from the committee rule's `minutes_review_business_days`), `NOTICE` (an unsuspended defence notice with a due date), `ACTION` (a corrective action's due date) and `SCHEDULE` (a meeting's start). A returned referral carries the structured reasons of the event that returned it, with the event type in `code`. A returned preliminary recommendation carries its `return_reason_codes`, to its preparer only. A comment written with a return never shows, and the My Work row shows the reasons as translated codes. ## Owner decisions on gap rows

Fady decided two gaps left after delivery on 2026-10-16 (CDF-248): AMB-113 (CDF-241, option B) and AMB-146 (CDF-246, option A). They settle how a gap is built, not how the row resolves, so they are not R5 decisions: no class, resolution, risk or test ID changes, and the coordinator placed the other six gap rows itself.

**Decision owner:** Fady, 2026-10-16 17:09 UTC, in the project chat (message cmsg_01CvgBgRZnhwQfVHsucbRcQyNUznhTAt7KyiDrEz5BwTfH), relayed by the coordinator at 17:10 UTC. The coordinator had put both questions to him after its placement ruling of 15:44 UTC. The message ends with a line on the merge order of PRs #94 and #95, both merged by then, which is not part of the decisions.

### AMB-113 (CDF-241): option B, "Wait for DoA source"

Register row AMB-113 (R1; class, resolution, risk and test ID unchanged). Status: decided on 2026-10-16, option B.

**Decision (Fady, verbatim), with an English rendering:**

> CDF-241 / AMB-113 → B — Wait for DoA source. لا نوسّع صلاحية الإحالة للإدارة القانونية إلى أي فئة جديدة بدون سند يحدد صاحب الصلاحية. يبقى Committee Secretary هو المستخدم الوحيد حاليًا، وتظل المسألة `SOURCE_REQUIRED` حتى ورود مصفوفة الصلاحيات/DoA أو تحديد الدور المعتمد. عندها يمكن تنفيذ التوسعة ضمن 2160. هذا أكثر اتساقًا مع مبدأ عدم توسيع الصلاحيات بالاستنتاج.

(CDF-241 / AMB-113 → B — Wait for DoA source. We do not widen the power to refer to the Legal department to any new group without a source that names who holds that power. The Committee Secretary stays the only user for now, and the matter stays `SOURCE_REQUIRED` until the authority matrix (DoA) arrives or the approved role is named. The widening can then be built within 2160. This is more consistent with the principle of not widening permissions by inference.)

**Placement:** Backlog, blocked on the source (the Linear title carries [BLOCKED: SOURCE_REQUIRED]); the widening ships in migration 2160 (ADR-053) once the Delegation of Authority or the approved role names who may refer.

**Until then:** The committee secretary stays the only caller of `api.request_legal_review` (1900, replaced in 2030 and in 2099 with the same caller check). Nothing is widened.

### AMB-146 (CDF-246): option A, "Nothing open"

Register row AMB-146 (R2; class, resolution, risk and test ID unchanged). Status: decided on 2026-10-16, option A.

**Decision (Fady, verbatim), with an English rendering:**

> CDF-246 / AMB-146 → A — Nothing open. القرار يصبح نهائيًا بما يكفي للإغلاق عندما لا يوجد طلب reconsideration قائم أو pending ولا يوجد مسار آخر يمنع الإغلاق. لا ننشئ مهلة افتراضية غير مسندة بمصدر. إذا قُدّم طلب reconsideration، يمنع الإغلاق حتى يُحسم؛ وإذا لم يوجد طلب مفتوح، فلا تبقى القضية معلقة نظريًا إلى أجل غير معلوم.

(CDF-246 / AMB-146 → A — Nothing open. A decision becomes final enough for closure when no reconsideration request is open or pending and no other path blocks closure. We do not create a default period that no source supports. If a reconsideration request is filed, it blocks closure until it is decided; and if no request is open, the case does not stay suspended, in theory, without end.)

> والصياغة التنفيذية لـCDF-246 تكون:
>
> `CASE_CLOSURE_FINALITY = no open or pending reconsideration, grievance, proceeding, or other configured closure blocker.`
>
> وجود حق reconsideration بحد ذاته لا يمنع الإغلاق ما لم يوجد طلب فعلي قائم أو مدة نظامية موثقة لاحقًا.

(And the executable wording for CDF-246 is: `CASE_CLOSURE_FINALITY = no open or pending reconsideration, grievance, proceeding, or other configured closure blocker.` A reconsideration right does not by itself block closure, unless an actual request is open or a statutory period is documented later.)

**Placement:** The closure hold needs no build: the closure guard of 2060 already refuses closure while a grievance or reconsideration is open (`DECISION_NOT_FINAL`). The derived finality read model (the GRIEVANCE_FINALITY flag and a field of the closure view, read under CASE_CLOSURE_FINALITY) is placed in migration 2170.

**Until then:** Closure is refused while a grievance or reconsideration is RECEIVED, AWAITING_ADMISSIBILITY_DECISION or ADMITTED (2060). An unused reconsideration right does not hold closure, and no default period exists.
